  run_end=$(date -u +%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S.%NZ)   bad=$(awk -F '\t' '$1=="bad" {print $2; exit}' "${prefix}-stats.txt" 2>/dev/null)   max_abs=$(awk -F '\t' '$1=="max_abs" {print $2; exit}' "${prefix}-stats.txt" 2>/dev/null)   [ -n "$bad" ] || bad=UNKNOWN   [ -n "$max_abs" ] || max_abs=UNKNOWN   printf 'RESULT variant=%s shape=128x%s dtype=FP32 rc=%s bad=%s max_abs=%s\nEND_UTC=%s\n' "$variant" "$width" "$rc" "$bad" "$max_abs" "$run_end" >> "$local_log"   printf 'rc=%s bad=%s max_abs=%s\n' "$rc" "$bad" "$max_abs" > "${prefix}-result.txt"   if [ "$rc" -ne 0 ] || [ "$bad" != 0 ]; then failed=1; fi } for width in 1020 1028 1036; do   for pair in 1 2 3 4 5 6; do     printf -v pair_id '%02d' "$pair"     snapshot_prefix="$base/v091-local-pair${pair_id}-r128-w${width}-fp32"     npu-smi info -t usages -i 4 -c 0 > "${snapshot_prefix}-pre-usage.txt" 2>&1     npu-smi info > "${snapshot_prefix}-pre-npu-smi-info.txt" 2>&1     ps -eo pid,etime,user,args > "${snapshot_prefix}-pre-processes.txt"     if [ $((pair % 2)) -eq 1 ]; then       run_one parent "$width"       run_one candidate "$width"     else       run_one candidate "$width"       run_one parent "$width"     fi     npu-smi info -t usages -i 4 -c 0 > "${snapshot_prefix}-post-usage.txt" 2>&1     npu-smi info > "${snapshot_prefix}-post-npu-smi-info.txt" 2>&1     ps -eo pid,etime,user,args > "${snapshot_prefix}-post-processes.txt"   done done end_utc=$(date -u +%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S.%NZ) printf 'END_UTC=%s\nRESULT=%s\n' "$end_utc" "$([ "$failed" -eq 0 ] && printf PASS || printf FAIL)" >> "$local_log" cat "$local_log" exit "$failed"
